00188: # Match recorded boxes using world-space bounds, independent of generated
00189: # mesh names or the material state created by SceneAct.
00190: material_cache = {}
00191: wood_meshes = []
00192: def material_for(color):
00193:     key = tuple(color)
00194:     if key not in material_cache:
00195:         mat = bpy.data.materials.new("Explicit finish")
00196:         mat.diffuse_color = (*color,1)
00197:         mat.use_nodes = True
00198:         shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00199:         shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color,1)
00200:         shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .55
00201:         material_cache[key] = mat
00202:     return material_cache[key]
00203: 
00204: for obj in bpy.data.objects:
00205:     if obj.type != "MESH":
00206:         continue
00207:     corners = [obj.matrix_world @ Vector(v) for v in obj.bound_box]
00208:     lo = Vector(tuple(min(v[k] for v in corners) for k in range(3)))
00209:     hi = Vector(tuple(max(v[k] for v in corners) for k in range(3)))
00210:     center = (lo+hi)/2
00211:     size = hi-lo
00212:     best = None
00213:     best_error = 1e9
00214:     for rec in box_finishes:
00215:         c,s,col,name = rec
00216:         error = sum(abs(center[k]-c[k])+abs(size[k]-s[k]) for k in range(3))
00217:         if error < best_error:
00218:             best_error = error
00219:             best = rec
00220:     if best is not None and best_error < .025:
00221:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00222:         obj.data.materials.append(material_for(best[2]))
00223:         if best[3] in ("foreground_post","right_post"):
00224:             wood_meshes.append(obj)
00225: 
00226: wood = bpy.data.materials.new("Longitudinal timber")
00227: wood.use_nodes = True
00228: nodes = wood.node_tree.nodes
00229: links = wood.node_tree.links
00230: shader = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00231: shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .69
00232: coords = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00233: mapping = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00234: mapping.operation = "MULTIPLY"
00235: mapping.inputs[1].default_value = (8,8,.32)
00236: noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00237: noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 4
00238: noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 3
00239: links.new(coords.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])

00211:     return max(abs(a[i]-b[i]) for i in range(3)) < .012

00213: # Read actual shader colors, with viewport colors as a fallback.
00214: # Customize shared finishes once; no dependence on generated mesh names.

00207: # Procedural surface finishes, without image textures.
00208: def material_nodes(mat):
00209:     mat.use_nodes = True
00210:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00211:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00212:     return nodes, links, nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00213: 
00214: def fabric_finish(mat):
00215:     nodes, links, bs = material_nodes(mat)
00216:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .88
00217:     tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00218:     sep = nodes.new("ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ")
00219:     links.new(tex.outputs["Generated"], sep.inputs[0])
00220:     wave = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00221:     wave.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00222:     wave.inputs[1].default_value = 170
00223:     links.new(sep.outputs["Y"], wave.inputs[0])
00224:     sine = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00225:     sine.operation = 'SINE'
00226:     links.new(wave.outputs[0], sine.inputs[0])
00227:     compare = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMath")
00228:     compare.operation = 'GREATER_THAN'
00229:     compare.inputs[1].default_value = .94
00230:     links.new(sine.outputs[0], compare.inputs[0])
00231:     mix = nodes.new("ShaderNodeMixRGB")
00232:     mix.inputs[1].default_value = (.79,.74,.65,1)
00233:     mix.inputs[2].default_value = (.52,.35,.31,1)
00234:     links.new(compare.outputs[0], mix.inputs[0])
00235:     links.new(mix.outputs[0], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00236:     noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00237:     noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 155
00238:     bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00239:     bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .15
00240:     bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .008
00241:     links.new(tex.outputs["Generated"], noise.inputs["Vector"])
00242:     links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00243:     links.new(bump.outputs[0], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00244: 
00245: # Identify finishes by original diffuse color, independent of object naming.
00246: used = set()
00247: for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
00248:     if obj.type != 'MESH':
00249:         continue
00250:     for slot in obj.material_slots:
00251:         mat = slot.material
00252:         if not mat or mat.name in used:
00253:             continue
00254:         used.add(mat.name)
00255:         rgb = tuple(mat.diffuse_color[:3])
00256:         if sum(abs(rgb[i]-fabric[i]) for i in range(3)) < .035:

00260:             if bs:

00295: 
00296: # Modest material roughness; a slightly reflective painted deck.
00297: for obj in bpy.data.objects:
00298:     if obj.type != 'MESH':
00299:         continue
00300:     for mat in obj.data.materials:
00301:         if mat is None:
00302:             continue
00303:         mat.use_nodes = True
00304:         shader = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00305:         if shader:
00306:             shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .69
00307:             base = shader.inputs["Base Color"].default_value
00308:             if base[0] > .9 and base[1] > .9 and base[2] > .8:
00309:                 if "Emission Color" in shader.inputs:
00310:                     shader.inputs["Emission Color"].default_value = (
00311:                         .9, .96, 1.0, 1)
00312:                     shader.inputs["Emission Strength"].default_value = 1.6
00313:             if .27 < base[0] < .35 and .10 < base[1] < .16:
00314:                 shader.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .38
